3. Can this text be classified as Uthmani, Imla'i or IndoPak?

Arabic prose / hadith sample

Not applicable as a Qur'anic edition label

Uthmani, Imla'i and IndoPak are labels used for Qur'anic text/edition representations. This sample is hadith prose; report its character encoding separately.

Perso-Arabic / Urdu-associated code points detected

ک U+06A9 ARABIC LETTER KEHEH, ہ U+06C1 ARABIC LETTER HEH GOAL, ۃ U+06C3 ARABIC LETTER TEH MARBUTA GOAL, ی U+06CC ARABIC LETTER FARSI YEH, ۔ U+06D4 ARABIC FULL STOP, ۴ U+06F4 EXTENDED ARABIC-INDIC DIGIT FOUR, ۶ U+06F6 EXTENDED ARABIC-INDIC DIGIT SIX, ۸ U+06F8 EXTENDED ARABIC-INDIC DIGIT EIGHT, ۹ U+06F9 EXTENDED ARABIC-INDIC DIGIT NINE

Evidence of extended Perso-Arabic character encoding. This does not prove the language is Urdu/Persian or that the text is an IndoPak Qur'an edition.

No strong Uthmani/Qur'anic annotation marker detected

No configured Uthmani-associated signs or Qur'anic annotation characters were found.

Absence is not proof of Imla'i; a plain text may have marks stripped or be only a fragment.

Reference-text comparison

For automatic identification, place the same passage from a known source in reference_texts/ using filenames such as uthmani.txt, imlaei.txt or indopak.txt. Similarity uses NFC-normalised text with whitespace/tatweel removed, so use the same passage/edition.

CandidateReference fileSimilarity
No reference files supplied.

Classification limitations

  • A font's name or glyph coverage cannot identify the orthography of a text rendered with it.
  • Perso-Arabic characters indicate codepoint usage, not necessarily Urdu or Persian language.
  • Imla'i cannot be established merely because Uthmani-specific characters are absent.
  • IndoPak needs source/edition matching or an authoritative reference; it is not synonymous with Urdu characters or Nastaliq font style.
  • A fixed Mushaf page layout (15-line/16-line/page number/line breaks) requires page and word positioning data, not a plain text string.

4. Font overview

Glyph count, Unicode cmap mappings and OpenType features measure different things. A cmap match does not prove good mark positioning; visual shaping should also be tested in a real browser.

Each record includes name-table metadata, copyright/licence strings when present, weight/width class, embedding flags, font metrics, Unicode block coverage, table inventory, GSUB/GPOS tags, variable-font axes and exact text coverage.

DescriptionArabic font for Holy Quran A high quality OpenType Font for Holy Quran Pakistan Data Management Services is pleased to announce the release of an OpenType Font for writing Holy Quran text based on Unicode standard. The font features high quality glyphs and has support for all aeraabs/mark placements at proper places. All waqfs (special symbols) used for identifying different pauses in reading are also provided. ‘PDMS Saleem Quran Font’ provides advanced Arabic typography - close to calligraphy while retaining the beauty and legibility of Quran Majeed Arabic text. This font is developed according to rules used in Quranic calligraphy in Pakistan, following the style of Yameen Dehlvi, who is one of the finest Quran calligraphers of Pakistan. Guidance and calligraphy of basic glyphs for the font has been provided by Mr. Mohammed Saleem. He is a student of Mohammed Iqbal Saeed and Yameen Dehlvi. Mohammed Saleem is also inspired by work of Shafiq uz Zaman (master calligrapher for Masjid Nabbawi) and frequently consults him for guidance. This font enables desktop and internet publishing of Holy Quran using existing software supporting OTF specifications, e.g. Microsoft Word, Microsoft Publisher, Internet Explorer, etc. ‘PDMS Saleem Quran Font’ is freely downloadable from www.pakdata.com/ArabicFont. We are confident that ‘PDMS Saleem Quran Font’ will appeal to all Arabic users, especially designers and publishers who will no longer be bound by the technological limitations of old Quran printing methods. About PDMS: Based in Karachi, Pakistan, PDMS is developing Urdu Software products since 1986. The nationally acclaimed ‘Nastaleeq Nizami’ – Urdu Desktop Publishing software has boosted tremendously the use of computers in Pakistan by making them write beautifully in Nastaleeq script of Urdu language. More products include Urdu Computer Nizam, Ruqqa-UrduEmail, Urdu 98, Urdu Mahir, PDMS Jauhar, Quran Majeed CD. PDMS has also developed fonts for the regional languages like Sindhi and Pushto besides designing a number of Nastaleeq and Naskh fonts. PDMS is also constantly engaged in developing Internet website and portals and has designed a number of Urdu portals including UrduSeek.com, UrduNews.net, CityIslam.com. The area in which PDMS has technical expertise makes it a unique venture in Pakistan. 6. CSS unicode-range declarations

Generated from each font's actual Unicode cmap. The ranges describe mapped code points, not a guarantee of correct shaping or a recommendation to load every font on one page.

A browser can still use fallback fonts for missing glyphs. Use the cmap coverage table when validating the selected font.

7. Orthography and page-layout reference

Orthography / text type: Uthmani and Imla'i are two Qur'anic writing conventions; IndoPak is a separately identified Qur'anic text representation, often paired with Nastaliq rendering. Typeface (Naskh/Nastaliq/Kufi) is a different property.

Mushaf page layout: a page format such as 15-line or 16-line requires page/line boundaries and verse/word positions. A plain continuous paragraph cannot reveal a printed Mushaf page layout.

  • Tanzil: Quran text types — Uthmani and Simple/Imla'i distinction.
  • Quran Foundation: field reference — separate `text_uthmani`, `text_imlaei` and `text_indopak_nastaleeq` fields.
  • Quran Foundation: page layout guide — distinct Mushaf IDs and page/line pagination.
  • Unicode Arabic names list — code point names and usage notes.
Generated by quran_font_analyzer.py. Font metadata and Unicode mappings were read from the supplied font binaries. Orthography classification is evidence-based and deliberately returns inconclusive when the input cannot support a reliable label.
